# riddim reference list, part 3: Bandcamp labels/artists, Free Music Archive (CC), samples (generated 2026-10-25).
# Same format as refs.txt: URL, tags, optional '# note'. Every Bandcamp URL was fetched 2026-10-25 (HTTP 200,
# title read from the page, >=1 streamable track; '[N tr]' = streamable tracks). Licence tag per line:
#   free-dl    = free / name-your-price on Bandcamp (minimum price 0)
#   study_only = paid release whose full stream is public: personal listening analysis only, never redistribute
#   cc-*       = Creative Commons licence stated on the Free Music Archive / ccMixter page
# First tag = family: from the label's genre, refined by the release's own Bandcamp tags (dubstep/grime/uk_garage).
# Label sections include releases on artists' own subdomains that the label page lists.
# Brief labels with no usable Bandcamp page: DMZ, Basic Channel / Rhythm & Sound, Wackies, Greensleeves (folded into VP),
# Iration Steppas, 2nd Drop, Locked On, Public Demand, Oil Gang, Basscamp (pages exist but list no releases),
# Hessle Audio and Hyperboloid (custom domains, not fetchable from here), Box Clever, Gorgon Sound, Truthoughts, Seven Seals,
# Dubquake, Ice Cream, Coyote (grime), Butterz/Kapsize/Black Box (subdomains belong to someone else), Baroque Dub,
# Echodub (now 'Late Echo', empty), Dub Revolutionaries; Jamendo pages are JS-only and could not be verified.
# URLs already in refs.txt / refs_more.txt are left out.
